Statement: The cutting plane with 76 rounds has an integrality gap of at least the Euler characteristic χ for Pastry.

Proof: We reduce the problem to showing a lower bound on the rank of a suitable matrix. By substitution method for CSP lower bounds, we establish that the support matrix has high rigidity, which implies the desired lower bound. The rank argument is completed via the expander mixing lemma. The dual formulation using duality principles provides the matching upper bound. ∎

Theorem 1001

Statement: The sum-of-squares hierarchy with 147 oracles has formula size of at least the chromatic number χ(G) for Petri Nets.

Proof: The proof proceeds by induction on the dimension. By random matrix theory for eigenvalue concentration, the base case is immediate from the classification theorem. The inductive step uses the exact sequence of the pair (X, A) and the long exact sequence in homology to lift the result. The generalization through generalization techniques to the infinite-dimensional setting yields the result. ∎
